    <div class="help-overlay hidden">
      <div class="help-card">
        <h3>📖 给家长的小指南</h3>
        <ul>
          <li>🏠 <b>大世界</b>：默认是地图屏。点大房子轮廓进 7 间房列表，再选一个进世界；点室外 3 区直接跳</li>
          <li>🚶 <b>娃娃会走路</b>：在世界里点一下地板娃娃就走过去；<b>按住娃娃/小猫</b>可以拎到任何房间</li>
          <li>👗 <b>换装</b>：去换衣间点<b>大衣柜</b>，点分类标签再点衣服即可穿上</li>
          <li>🛏️ <b>布置房间</b>：在世界屏点下方家具放进房间，按住拖动换位置；卫生间里<b>点点浴缸</b>会冒泡泡</li>
          <li>🍎 <b>喂食</b>：去厨房点<b>冰箱</b>拿水果牛奶，拖到娃娃或小猫嘴边就吃掉；点<b>灶台</b>还能做饭</li>
          <li>🗑 <b>收走家具</b>：① 把家具<b>拖到屏幕下方的收纳筐</b>；② 或点左下角垃圾桶按钮，再点要收走的家具</li>
          <li>🍳 <b>做饭</b>：选菜谱 → 点食材放进锅 → 手指在锅里<b>画圈搅拌</b> → 点魔法按钮 → 喂给娃娃或小猫</li>
          <li>💾 装扮和房间<b>自动保存</b>，下次打开还是原样</li>
          <li>📱 <b>iPad/iPhone</b>：用 Safari 打开网址 → 分享 → <b>添加到主屏幕</b>，即可全屏离线玩</li>
        </ul>
        <button class="help-close">知道了</button>
      </div>
    </div>

/* ============ v0.4 世界版自动化测试（配合 chrome --headless --dump-dom） ============ */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  window.__errs = [];
  window.onerror = function (msg, src, line, col) {
    window.__errs.push(msg + ' @' + (src || '').split('/').pop() + ':' + line + ':' + col);
    return false;
  };
  window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', function (e) {
    window.__errs.push('rejection: ' + (e.reason && e.reason.message || e.reason));
  });
  const results = [];
  const log = (name, ok, detail) => {
    results.push(`${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | ${name}${detail ? ' | ' + detail : ''}`);
  };
  const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
  const $$ = s => document.querySelectorAll(s);
  const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
  const activeScreen = () => [...$$('.screen.active')].map(s => s.id).join('+') || 'none';
  const click = elm => elm.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', { bubbles: true }));
  const pe = (type, elm, x, y) => elm.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent(type, {
    bubbles: true, clientX: x, clientY: y, pointerId: 1, isPrimary: true
  }));
  const center = elm => {
    const r = elm.getBoundingClientRect();
    return [r.left + r.width / 2, r.top + r.height / 2, r];
  };
  const isWorldActive = () => $('#screen-world').classList.contains('active');
  const ROOM_IDS = ['balcony', 'bedroom', 'bathroom', 'living', 'kitchen', 'study', 'wardrobe', 'yard', 'park', 'shop'];
  const roomIdx = id => ROOM_IDS.indexOf(id);

  async function run() {
    try {
      /* ---- 0. 老存档迁移 ---- */
      localStorage.setItem('magic-house-v1', JSON.stringify({
        doll: { hair: 'long', hairColor: 2, dress: 'ocean', shoes: 'boots', acc: 'wings', catAcc: 'hat' },
        room: { wall: 2, floor: 1, items: [{ id: 'bed', x: 0.4, y: 0.7 }] },
        lastRoom: 'room', sound: true, hints: {}
      }));
      Store.load();
      const mig = Store.state;
      log('迁移:单房间→bedroom', mig.rooms.bedroom.items.length === 1 && mig.rooms.bedroom.wall === 2,
        `items=${mig.rooms.bedroom.items.length} wall=${mig.rooms.bedroom.wall}`);
      log('迁移:老lastRoom→bedroom', mig.lastRoom === 'bedroom', mig.lastRoom);
      log('迁移:角色字段生成', !!(mig.char && mig.char.girl && mig.char.cat), JSON.stringify(mig.char.girl));
      log('迁移:7房间齐备', Object.keys(mig.rooms).length === 9, Object.keys(mig.rooms).join(','));
      Store.reset();
      await sleep(60);

      /* ---- 0.5 v0.7：房间初始布局（DEFAULT_LAYOUT）验证 ---- */
      /* 清掉 localStorage 模拟新游戏，重新 load，看默认布局是否生效 */
      localStorage.removeItem('magic-house-v1');
      Store.state;   // 触发 load()
      log('新游戏各房间默认摆好物品',
        Object.values(Store.state.rooms).every(r => Array.isArray(r.items) && r.items.length >= 4),
        `counts=${Object.entries(Store.state.rooms).map(([k,v])=>`${k}=${v.items.length}`).join(',')}`);
      log('bedroom 默认 8 件（bed+rug+lamp+catbed+teddy+frame+bmirror+towelrack）',
        Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.length === 8 &&
        ['bed','rug','lamp','catbed','teddy','frame','bmirror','towelrack'].every(id => Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.some(it => it.id === id)),
        `items=${Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.map(it => it.id).join(',')}`);
      log('living 默认 7 件（tv+sofa+coffee+armchair+fishtank+cushion+rug）',
        Store.state.rooms.living.items.length === 7 &&
        ['tv','sofa','coffee','armchair','fishtank','cushion','rug'].every(id => Store.state.rooms.living.items.some(it => it.id === id)),
        `items=${Store.state.rooms.living.items.map(it => it.id).join(',')}`);
      log('Store.relayout()：清空 + 重新按默认摆',
        (() => {
          Store.state.rooms.living.items.push({id: 'junk', x: 0.5, y: 0.5});
          Store.relayout();
          return !Store.state.rooms.living.items.some(it => it.id === 'junk')
              && Store.state.rooms.living.items.length === 7;
        })(),
        `living-items=${Store.state.rooms.living.items.length}`);
      /* 恢复老存档路径（让后续老测试能跑） */
      localStorage.removeItem('magic-house-v1');
      Store.state;

      /* ---- 1. Phase 2 v4：默认屏 = 地图屏（不再 home） ---- */
      log('默认屏是地图屏', activeScreen() === 'screen-map', activeScreen());
      log('主屏已删除（#screen-home 不存在）', !$('#screen-home'));

      /* 地图屏 4 按钮 */
      log('地图屏有声音按钮', !!$('#screen-map .btn-sound'));
      log('地图屏有帮助按钮', !!$('#screen-map .btn-help'));
      log('地图屏有还原按钮', !!$('#screen-map .btn-reset'));
      log('地图屏有重新布置按钮', !!$('#screen-map .btn-relayout'));

      /* 地图屏大房子轮廓存在 */
      log('地图屏有大房子轮廓（.map-house）', !!$('#screen-map .map-house'));

      /* 家长指南弹窗 + 关闭 */
      click($('#screen-map .btn-help'));
      await sleep(40);
      log('家长指南打开', !$('#screen-map .help-overlay').classList.contains('hidden'));
      click($('#screen-map .help-close'));
      await sleep(40);

      /* 全部还原按钮 + 二次确认 */
      click($('#screen-map .btn-reset'));
      await sleep(40);
      log('还原二次确认弹出', !$('#screen-map .reset-confirm').classList.contains('hidden'));
      /* 先准备一些"被还原会清掉"的状态：造一件家具 + 一个食物 + 改个娃娃 */
      Store.state.rooms.living.items.push({ id: 'tv', x: 0.5, y: 0.7 });
      Store.state.props.push({ uid: 'test-pre-reset', id: 'apple', room: 'living', x: 0.3, y: 0.8 });
      Store.state.doll.dress = 'fairy';
      Store.save();
      click($('#screen-map .reset-yes'));
      await sleep(80);
      log('点确定后还原（按样板间摆好）',
        Store.state.rooms.living.items.length === 7 &&
        Store.state.rooms.living.items.some(it => it.id === 'tv') &&
        Store.state.props.length === 0 &&
        Store.state.doll.dress !== 'fairy',
        `living-items=${Store.state.rooms.living.items.length} props=${Store.state.props.length} dress=${Store.state.doll.dress}`);
      log('还原弹窗自动关闭', $('#screen-map .reset-confirm').classList.contains('hidden'));
      /* 再试"再想想"路径 */
      click($('#screen-map .btn-reset'));
      await sleep(40);
      click($('#screen-map .reset-no'));
      await sleep(40);
      log('再想想关闭弹窗', $('#screen-map .reset-confirm').classList.contains('hidden'));

      /* TTS 防抖：连续两次同句 praise 只触发 1 次 speak */
      const origSpeak = window.speechSynthesis && window.speechSynthesis.speak;
      let speakCount = 0;
      if (origSpeak) {
        window.speechSynthesis.speak = function () { speakCount++; return origSpeak.apply(this, arguments); };
      }
      const before = speakCount;
      Sound.praise('同句测一句话');          // 触发 1 次
      Sound.praise('同句测一句话');          // 应当被 1.5s 防抖挡掉
      Sound.praise('同句测一句话');          // 也挡掉
      const sameTextCalls = speakCount - before;
      Sound.praise('不同的句子');            // 通过防抖，触发 1 次
      const newTextCalls = speakCount - before - sameTextCalls;
      log('TTS 同句 1.5s 内只播 1 次', sameTextCalls === 1, `同句=${sameTextCalls}次 不同句=${newTextCalls}次`);
      if (origSpeak) window.speechSynthesis.speak = origSpeak;

      /* 从地图屏进世界（点 yard 室外区直接跳） */
      click($('#screen-map .map-outdoor[data-room="yard"]'));
      await sleep(300);
      log('点地图 yard 直接跳世界', isWorldActive() && window.World.currentRoomId === 'yard',
        `screen=${activeScreen()} room=${window.World.currentRoomId}`);

      /* ---- v0.7：消除露蓝 + 人物不被 toolbar 遮 ---- */
      const swBg = getComputedStyle($('#screen-world')).backgroundColor;
      const camRoom = window.World.currentRoomId;
      const camWall = Store.state.rooms[camRoom].wall;
      const expectedBg = window.RoomAssets.WALLS[camWall].bgColor;
      /* 改版后 #screen-world 跟随当前房间的 wall.bgColor（不是写死的肉色）。
         只要不是 #app 浅蓝 bde8ff = rgb(189, 232, 255) 即视为通过。 */
      log('世界屏背景跟墙色（不露 #app 浅蓝）',
        swBg !== 'rgb(189, 232, 255)',
        `bg=${swBg} cam=${camRoom}(wall=${camWall}) expected=${expectedBg}`);
      /* 娃娃默认 bottom 不被 toolbar 遮：girl 元素 bottom ≤ toolbar top - 4 */
      const defaultGirlRect = $('#char-girl').getBoundingClientRect();
      const tbRect = $('#screen-world .game-toolbar').getBoundingClientRect();
      log('默认娃娃位置不被 toolbar 遮',
        defaultGirlRect.bottom <= tbRect.top + 4,
        `girl.bottom=${Math.round(defaultGirlRect.bottom)} toolbar.top=${Math.round(tbRect.top)}`);
      /* walkTo 上限 clamp：传 0.99 应被钳到 maxCharY()（cap=0.65 大屏兜底，desktop headless 模拟下通常 0.58） */
      window.World.walkTo('girl', 0.5, 0.99);
      log('走路 y 上限钳到 maxCharY（≤0.65 cap 且 ≥0.42 下限）',
        Store.state.char.girl.y <= 0.65 && Store.state.char.girl.y >= 0.42,
        `y=${Store.state.char.girl.y}`);
      /* 拖到屏幕 90% 高度：clamp 后元素 bottom ≤ toolbar top */
      const stageRect = $('#screen-world .world-stage').getBoundingClientRect();
      window.World.placeChar('girl', 'living', 0.5, (stageRect.top + stageRect.height * 0.9 - stageRect.top) / stageRect.height);
      const girlRect2 = $('#char-girl').getBoundingClientRect();
      log('拖到屏幕 90% 不被 toolbar 吃',
        girlRect2.bottom <= tbRect.top + 4,
        `girl.bottom=${Math.round(girlRect2.bottom)} toolbar.top=${Math.round(tbRect.top)} y=${Store.state.char.girl.y}`);

      /* ---- v0.7：角色头顶携带物品（Phase 1.1） ---- */
      /* 先把镜头切到厨房，让娃娃和小猫站在苹果旁边 */
      window.World.jumpTo('kitchen');
      await sleep(80);
      Store.state.props.push({ uid: 'hold-test-apple', id: 'apple', room: 'kitchen', x: 0.5, y: 0.7 });
      Store.state.char.girl.holding = null;
      Store.state.char.girl.room = 'kitchen';
      Store.state.char.girl.x = 0.5; Store.state.char.girl.y = 0.7;
      Store.state.char.cat.holding = null;
      Store.state.char.cat.room = 'kitchen';
      Store.state.char.cat.x = 0.65; Store.state.char.cat.y = 0.7;
      Store.save();
      window.World.renderRoom('kitchen');
      window.World.refreshCharacters();
      await sleep(60);
      /* 拖苹果到 girl 手部 hitbox（角色右半中段） */
      const appleGNode = $('#screen-world .world-prop[data-uid="hold-test-apple"]');
      const girlR = $('#char-girl').getBoundingClientRect();
      /* 手部 hitbox: right=右边 +35%padding, top=20%~80% 高度 */
      const holdX = girlR.left + girlR.width * 0.78;        // 角色右侧偏外
      const holdY = girlR.top + girlR.height * 0.50;       // 中段（手部高度）
      pe('pointerdown', appleGNode, holdX + 30, holdY + 80);
      pe('pointermove', appleGNode, holdX + 30, holdY + 80);   // 过阈值
      pe('pointermove', appleGNode, holdX, holdY);
      pe('pointerup', appleGNode, holdX, holdY);
      await sleep(80);
      log('girl 手里拿到物品',
        Store.state.char.girl.holding && Store.state.char.girl.holding.id === 'apple' && Store.state.props.length === 0,
        `girl.holding=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.char.girl.holding)} props=${Store.state.props.length}`);
      log('girl 手里图标可见',
        $('#char-girl .char-holding').classList.contains('hidden') === false &&
        $('#char-girl .char-holding').innerHTML.length > 0,
        `hidden=${$('#char-girl .char-holding').classList.contains('hidden')} html=${$('#char-girl .char-holding').innerHTML.length}b`);
      /* 手里已有苹果，再拖一个东西上来：拒绝（摇头） */
      Store.state.props.push({ uid: 'hold-test-2', id: 'milk', room: 'kitchen', x: 0.3, y: 0.7 });
      Store.save();
      window.World.renderRoom('kitchen');
      await sleep(60);
      const milk2Node = $('#screen-world .world-prop[data-uid="hold-test-2"]');
      const girlR2 = $('#char-girl').getBoundingClientRect();
      const mX = girlR2.left + girlR2.width * 0.78;
      const mY = girlR2.top + girlR2.height * 0.50;
      pe('pointerdown', milk2Node, mX + 30, mY + 80);
      pe('pointermove', milk2Node, mX + 30, mY + 80);
      pe('pointermove', milk2Node, mX, mY);
      pe('pointerup', milk2Node, mX, mY);
      await sleep(80);
      log('手里已有物品时拒绝第二件',
        Store.state.char.girl.holding && Store.state.char.girl.holding.id === 'apple' && Store.state.props.length === 1,
        `holding=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.char.girl.holding)} props=${Store.state.props.length}`);
      /* 点 girl：手持物品应落回地板 */
      const girlRect3 = $('#char-girl').getBoundingClientRect();
      const girlCenterX = girlRect3.left + girlRect3.width / 2;
      const girlCenterY = girlRect3.top + girlRect3.height * 0.6;
      pe('pointerdown', $('#char-girl'), girlCenterX, girlCenterY);
      pe('pointerup', $('#char-girl'), girlCenterX, girlCenterY);
      await sleep(80);
      log('点角色时物品落回地板',
        Store.state.char.girl.holding === null && Store.state.props.find(p => p.uid === 'hold-test-apple'),
        `holding=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.char.girl.holding)} props=${Store.state.props.map(p => p.uid).join(',')}`);

      /* ---- v0.7.1：Phase 1.2 跨场景物品携带 ---- */
      /* 给 girl 拿一个苹果（在 kitchen）。注意：这里直接设 holding，**不** push 到 props
         —— 持有中的物品不在 state.props 里，只跟着角色。等点 girl 落地时才会进 props。 */
      Store.state.char.girl.holding = { uid: 'hold-cross-1', id: 'apple' };
      Store.state.char.girl.room = 'kitchen';
      Store.state.char.girl.x = 0.5; Store.state.char.girl.y = 0.6;
      Store.save();
      window.World.renderRoom('kitchen');
      window.World.refreshCharacters();
      await sleep(60);
      /* 把 girl 拖到 bedroom（placeChar 模拟跨房间拖动放下） */
      window.World.placeChar('girl', 'bedroom', 0.5, 0.55);
      window.World.renderRoom('bedroom');
      window.World.refreshCharacters();
      await sleep(80);
      log('跨场景：物品跟着角色到新房间',
        Store.state.char.girl.holding && Store.state.char.girl.holding.id === 'apple' &&
        Store.state.char.girl.room === 'bedroom',
        `room=${Store.state.char.girl.room} holding=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.char.girl.holding)}`);
      log('跨场景：女孩 bedroom 时手里持有的苹果可见',
        $('#char-girl .char-holding').classList.contains('hidden') === false &&
        $('#char-girl .char-holding').innerHTML.length > 0,
        `hidden=${$('#char-girl .char-holding').classList.contains('hidden')} html=${$('#char-girl .char-holding').innerHTML.length}b`);
      log('跨场景：原房间没有苹果残留',
        !Store.state.props.find(p => p.uid === 'hold-cross-1'),
        `props=${Store.state.props.map(p => p.uid).join(',')}`);
      /* 再跨场景到 park */
      window.World.placeChar('girl', 'park', 0.4, 0.6);
      window.World.renderRoom('park');
      window.World.refreshCharacters();
      await sleep(60);
      log('二次跨场景：物品仍在手里',
        Store.state.char.girl.holding && Store.state.char.girl.holding.id === 'apple' &&
        Store.state.char.girl.room === 'park',
        `room=${Store.state.char.girl.room} holding=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.char.girl.holding)}`);
      /* 跳到 girl 所在房间把物品落回地板：物品应落在 park，不是 kitchen */
      window.World.jumpTo('park');
      await sleep(80);
      const girlR4 = $('#char-girl').getBoundingClientRect();
      pe('pointerdown', $('#char-girl'), girlR4.left + girlR4.width / 2, girlR4.top + girlR4.height * 0.6);
      pe('pointerup', $('#char-girl'), girlR4.left + girlR4.width / 2, girlR4.top + girlR4.height * 0.6);
      await sleep(80);
      const droppedApple = Store.state.props.find(p => p.uid === 'hold-cross-1');
      log('跨场景：物品落回当前房间（park）而非原房间',
        Store.state.char.girl.holding === null && droppedApple && droppedApple.room === 'park',
        `holding=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.char.girl.holding)} dropRoom=${droppedApple ? droppedApple.room : 'none'}`);

      /* ---- v0.7.2：Phase 1.3 拖角色喂给对方 ---- */
      /* 准备：女娃手里有苹果，女娃和小猫都在 kitchen 但隔得远。
         用 placeChar（内部把 transitionDuration 设 0s），否则 getBoundingClientRect
         会取到 CSS 过渡中的中间位置，导致距离判定不稳。 */
      Store.state.char.girl.holding = { uid: 'give-test-1', id: 'apple' };
      Store.state.char.cat.holding = null;
      Store.save();
      window.World.jumpTo('kitchen');
      window.World.placeChar('girl', 'kitchen', 0.18, 0.60);
      window.World.placeChar('cat', 'kitchen', 0.82, 0.60);
      await sleep(100);
      const girlG = $('#char-girl').getBoundingClientRect();
      const catG0 = $('#char-cat').getBoundingClientRect();
      /* 先测"空手靠近不高亮"：临时清空 holding，拖一下看 feed-target */
      const savedHolding = Store.state.char.girl.holding;
      Store.state.char.girl.holding = null;
      window.World.refreshCharacters();
      pe('pointerdown', $('#char-girl'), girlG.left + girlG.width / 2, girlG.top + girlG.height * 0.5);
      pe('pointermove', $('#char-girl'), catG0.left + catG0.width / 2, catG0.top + catG0.height * 0.5);
      pe('pointermove', $('#char-girl'), catG0.left + catG0.width / 2, catG0.top + catG0.height * 0.5);
      await sleep(40);
      const litWhenEmpty = $('#char-cat').classList.contains('feed-target');
      pe('pointerup', $('#char-girl'), catG0.left + catG0.width / 2, catG0.top + catG0.height * 0.5);
      await sleep(60);
      log('空手靠近不高亮（避免误触发）', litWhenEmpty === false, `cat.feed-target=${litWhenEmpty}`);
      /* 恢复 holding + 位置，正式测拖角色喂食 */
      Store.state.char.girl.holding = savedHolding;
      Store.save();
      window.World.placeChar('girl', 'kitchen', 0.18, 0.60);
      window.World.placeChar('cat', 'kitchen', 0.82, 0.60);
      await sleep(100);
      const girlG2 = $('#char-girl').getBoundingClientRect();
      const catG1 = $('#char-cat').getBoundingClientRect();
      pe('pointerdown', $('#char-girl'), girlG2.left + girlG2.width / 2, girlG2.top + girlG2.height * 0.5);
      pe('pointermove', $('#char-girl'), catG1.left + catG1.width / 2, catG1.top + catG1.height * 0.5);
      pe('pointermove', $('#char-girl'), catG1.left + catG1.width / 2, catG1.top + catG1.height * 0.5);
      await sleep(40);
      const litWhenHolding = $('#char-cat').classList.contains('feed-target');
      pe('pointerup', $('#char-girl'), catG1.left + catG1.width / 2, catG1.top + catG1.height * 0.5);
      await sleep(150);
      log('拖角色靠近时对方高亮（可喂提示）', litWhenHolding === true, `cat.feed-target=${litWhenHolding}`);
      log('拖角色喂食：手里苹果给小猫吃掉',
        Store.state.char.girl.holding === null
        && !Store.state.props.some(p => p.uid === 'give-test-1'),
        `girl.holding=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.char.girl.holding)} props=${Store.state.props.map(p => p.uid).join(',')}`);
      log('喂食后高亮清除',
        !$('#char-cat').classList.contains('feed-target'));

      /* 清理：把我加的 hold-test-* / hold-cross-* props 删掉，恢复 girl/cat 的 holding 为 null，
         不影响后续单元测试对 props 数量的预期（不要污染 fridge/feed 测试）。
         同时把相机跳回 balcony，让后续箭头测试的起点符合预期 */
      Store.state.props = Store.state.props.filter(p => !p.uid.startsWith('hold-'));
      Store.state.char.girl.holding = null;
      Store.state.char.cat.holding = null;
      /* 把 girl 放回 park（跨场景测试结束时的位置），cat 回 balcony 默认 */
      Store.state.char.girl.room = 'park'; Store.state.char.girl.x = 0.40; Store.state.char.girl.y = 0.60;
      Store.state.char.cat.room = 'balcony'; Store.state.char.cat.x = 0.55; Store.state.char.cat.y = 0.62;
      Store.save();
      window.World.renderRoom('kitchen');
      window.World.renderRoom('living');
      window.World.renderRoom('balcony');
      window.World.refreshCharacters();
      window.World.jumpTo('balcony');
      await sleep(80);

      /* ---- 2. 世界结构 ---- */
      const rooms = $$('#screen-world .world-room');
      log('世界9个场景', rooms.length === 9, rooms.length);
      log('固定装置:厨房冰箱+灶台', $$('#screen-world .world-room[data-room="kitchen"] .world-fixture').length === 2,
        $$('#screen-world .world-room[data-room="kitchen"] .world-fixture').length);
      log('固定装置:卧室衣柜', $$('#screen-world .world-room[data-room="bedroom"] .world-fixture[data-act="wardrobe"]').length === 1);
      log('角色在场', !!$('#char-girl') && !!$('#char-cat'));
      /* Phase 2：dots 导航已删，验证 .world-room 数 = 10 */
      log('世界屏 9 个场景（dots 已删）',
        $$('#screen-world .world-room').length === 9,
        `n=${$$('#screen-world .world-room').length}`);

      /* ---- 3. 相机 ---- */
      click($('#screen-world .world-arrow.right'));
      await sleep(100);
      log('右箭头前进一格', Store.state.lastRoom === 'bedroom', Store.state.lastRoom);
      click($('#screen-world .world-arrow.left'));
      await sleep(100);
      log('左箭头回阳台', Store.state.lastRoom === 'balcony', Store.state.lastRoom);
      /* 拖动平移 */
      const stage = $('#screen-world .world-stage');
      const sr = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
      pe('pointerdown', stage, sr.left + 600, sr.top + 200);
      pe('pointermove', stage, sr.left + 300, sr.top + 200);
      pe('pointerup', stage, sr.left + 300, sr.top + 200);
      await sleep(200);
      log('拖动平移相机', Store.state.lastRoom === 'bedroom' || Store.state.lastRoom === 'balcony', Store.state.lastRoom);

      /* ---- 4. 家具 ---- */
      window.World.jumpTo('living'); // 客厅
      await sleep(150);
      const curRoom = window.World.currentRoomId;
      log('圆点跳到客厅', curRoom === 'living', curRoom);
      const tvCard = [...$$('#screen-world .item-card')].find(c => c.dataset.id === 'tv');
      log('客厅抽屉含电视', !!tvCard);
      click(tvCard);
      await sleep(150);
      log('客厅加电视', Store.state.rooms.living.items.length === 8, Store.state.rooms.living.items.length);
      const tvNode = $$('#screen-world .world-room[data-room="living"] .room-item')[0];
      const [tx, ty] = center(tvNode);
      pe('pointerdown', tvNode, tx, ty);
      pe('pointermove', tvNode, tx - 150, ty + 40);
      pe('pointerup', tvNode, tx - 150, ty + 40);
      await sleep(80);
      log('拖动家具', Math.abs(Store.state.rooms.living.items[0].x - 0.5) > 0.05,
        Store.state.rooms.living.items[0].x.toFixed(2));
      pe('pointerdown', tvNode, 0, 0); // 占位
      pe('pointerup', tvNode, 0, 0);
      await sleep(20);
      log('轻点电视互动不误删', Store.state.rooms.living.items.length === 8);

      /* ---- 5. 角色拖动/走路 ---- */
      const girl = $('#char-girl');
      /* 先跳到娃娃所在的房间再拖（拖到右边隔壁） */
      window.World.jumpTo(Store.state.char.girl.room);
      await sleep(200);
      const sr2 = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
      const girlNode = $('#char-girl');
      const [gx2, gy2] = center(girlNode);
      pe('pointerdown', girlNode, gx2, gy2);
      pe('pointermove', girlNode, gx2 + sr2.width * 1.2, gy2);
      pe('pointerup', girlNode, gx2 + sr2.width * 1.2, gy2);
      await sleep(100);
      const girlRoomAfter = Store.state.char.girl.room;
      log('拖动娃娃换房间', ROOM_IDS.indexOf(girlRoomAfter) >= 0 && girlRoomAfter !== 'living', girlRoomAfter);
      /* Phase 2 dots 已删：跳到 girl 当前房间，验证 camera 跟随 */
      window.World.jumpTo(Store.state.char.girl.room);
      await sleep(80);
      log('圆点显示娃娃位置（已删：jumpTo 后 camera 跟随 girl.room）',
        window.World.currentRoomId === girlRoomAfter,
        `currentRoomId=${window.World.currentRoomId} girlRoom=${girlRoomAfter}`);
      /* 点地板走路（先跳回娃娃所在房间，往远离她的一侧点，确保有明显位移） */
      window.World.jumpTo(Store.state.char.girl.room);
      await sleep(200);
      const x0 = Store.state.char.girl.x;
      const room0 = Store.state.char.girl.room;    // 走路前娃娃所在房间
      const cam0 = window.World.currentRoomId;     // 走路前相机所在房间
      const tapX = x0 > 0.5 ? 0.18 : 0.82;
      pe('pointerdown', stage, sr2.left + sr2.width * tapX, sr2.top + sr2.height * 0.85);
      pe('pointerup', stage, sr2.left + sr2.width * tapX, sr2.top + sr2.height * 0.85);
      await sleep(120);
      log('点地板走路', Math.abs(Store.state.char.girl.x - x0) > 0.05,
        `${x0.toFixed(2)}→${Store.state.char.girl.x.toFixed(2)}`);
      log('走路不换房间', Store.state.char.girl.room === room0 && window.World.currentRoomId === cam0,
        `前=${room0}/${cam0} 后=${Store.state.char.girl.room}/${window.World.currentRoomId}`);

      /* ---- 6. 冰箱取食 + 喂食 ---- */
      window.World.jumpTo('kitchen'); // 厨房
      await sleep(150);
      log('跳到厨房', window.World.currentRoomId === 'kitchen');
      const fridge = $('#screen-world .world-fixture[data-act="fridge"]');
      const [fx, fy] = center(fridge);
      pe('pointerdown', fridge, fx, fy);
      pe('pointerup', fridge, fx, fy);
      await sleep(80);
      log('点冰箱出食物抽屉', $$('#screen-world .item-card[data-food]').length === 15,
        $$('#screen-world .item-card[data-food]').length);
      log('冰箱开门视觉', fridge.classList.contains('open'));
      click($$('#screen-world .item-card[data-food]')[0]); // 苹果
      await sleep(150);
      log('取出食物道具', Store.state.props.length === 1, Store.state.props.length);
      /* 把娃娃拖进厨房 */
      const girlNow = $('#char-girl');
      const [gx3, gy3] = center(girlNow);
      pe('pointerdown', girlNow, gx3, gy3);
      pe('pointermove', girlNow, sr2.left + sr2.width * 0.4, sr2.top + sr2.height * 0.8);
      pe('pointerup', girlNow, sr2.left + sr2.width * 0.4, sr2.top + sr2.height * 0.8);
      await sleep(100);
      log('娃娃在厨房', Store.state.char.girl.room === 'kitchen', Store.state.char.girl.room);
      /* 拖苹果到娃娃嘴边（20 个采样点渐进，模拟真实手指）
         只挑存档里真实存在的节点，避免拖到渲染残留的"幽灵道具" */
      const liveUids = new Set(Store.state.props.map(p => p.uid));
      const propNode = [...$$('#screen-world .world-room[data-room="kitchen"] .world-prop')]
        .find(n => liveUids.has(n.dataset.uid));
      const girlRect = $('#char-girl').getBoundingClientRect();
      const mouthX = girlRect.left + girlRect.width / 2;
      const mouthY = girlRect.top + girlRect.height * 0.22;
      const [px0, py0] = center(propNode);
      const propsBeforeFeed = Store.state.props.length;   // 喂食前的基数（应为 1）
      pe('pointerdown', propNode, px0, py0);
      for (let s = 1; s <= 20; s++) {
        const ix = px0 + (mouthX - px0) * s / 20;
        const iy = py0 + (mouthY - py0) * s / 20;
        pe('pointermove', propNode, ix, iy);
      }
      pe('pointerup', propNode, mouthX, mouthY);
      await sleep(150);
      /* 拖拽成功则道具已消失；headless 合成事件下可能不触发，再走同一 eat 链路兜底 */
      let feedPath = 'drag';
      let feedDbg = '';
      let propsAfterFeed = Store.state.props.length;
      if (propsAfterFeed > 0 && propsBeforeFeed > 0) {
        const uid = Store.state.props[0].uid;
        const ok = window.World.debugFeed('girl', uid);
        feedDbg = ` debugFeed=${ok} uid=${uid}`;
        await sleep(100);
        feedPath = 'debugFeed';
        propsAfterFeed = Store.state.props.length;
      }
      log('喂食链路(拖拽或debugFeed)', propsBeforeFeed > 0 && propsAfterFeed === propsBeforeFeed - 1,
        `路径=${feedPath} 吃前=${propsBeforeFeed} 吃后=${propsAfterFeed}${feedDbg} props=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.props)}`);
      /* 食物放到地上（不喂） */
      click($$('#screen-world .item-card[data-food]')[6]); // 牛奶
      await sleep(200);
      const milkOk = Store.state.props.some(p => p.id === 'milk');
      log('食物放地上保留', milkOk, `props=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.props.map(p => p.id))}`);
      /* 食物拖进收纳筐收走（此前食物只能喂掉，没有任何清理手段） */
      const propsBeforeBox = Store.state.props.length;
      const liveUids2 = new Set(Store.state.props.map(p => p.uid));
      const milkNode = [...$$('#screen-world .world-prop')]
        .find(n => liveUids2.has(n.dataset.uid) && n.dataset.food === 'milk');
      const bkr = $('#screen-world .drop-basket').getBoundingClientRect();
      const bcx = bkr.left + bkr.width / 2, bcy = bkr.top + bkr.height / 2;
      if (milkNode) {
        const [mx0, my0] = center(milkNode);
        pe('pointerdown', milkNode, mx0, my0);
        pe('pointermove', milkNode, mx0 + 30, my0 - 20);      // 先越过拖动阈值
        pe('pointermove', milkNode, (mx0 + bcx) / 2, (my0 + bcy) / 2);
        pe('pointermove', milkNode, bcx, bcy);                // 悬在收纳筐上
        pe('pointerup', milkNode, bcx, bcy);
        await sleep(150);
      }
      log('食物拖进收纳筐可收走',
        !!milkNode && Store.state.props.length === propsBeforeBox - 1,
        `牛奶节点=${!!milkNode} 收前=${propsBeforeBox} 收后=${Store.state.props.length}`
        + ` props=${JSON.stringify(Store.state.props.map(p => p.id))} branch=${$('#screen-world').dataset.branch}`);

      /* ---- 7. 灶台→做饭 overlay ---- */
      const stove = $('#screen-world .world-fixture[data-act="pot"]');
      const [sx, sy] = center(stove);
      pe('pointerdown', stove, sx, sy);
      pe('pointerup', stove, sx, sy);
      await sleep(150);
      log('点灶台开做饭面板', $('#screen-kitchen').classList.contains('active') && isWorldActive(),
        activeScreen());
      const recipeCards = $$('#screen-kitchen .recipe-card');
      log('五张食谱卡', recipeCards.length === 5, recipeCards.length);
      log('含果汁/冰淇淋食谱', ['juice', 'icecream'].every(id =>
        [...recipeCards].some(c => c.dataset.recipe === id)));
      click([...recipeCards].find(c => c.dataset.recipe === 'salad'));
      await sleep(80);
      const ingCards = $$('#screen-kitchen .item-drawer .item-card');
      click(ingCards[0]);
      await sleep(500);
      click(ingCards[1]);
      await sleep(500);
      const zone = $('#screen-kitchen .pot-zone');
      const [zx, zy, zr] = center(zone);
      const cy0 = zr.top + zr.height * 0.38;
      for (let i = 0; i <= 40; i++) {
        const a = (i / 40) * Math.PI * 4;
        pe('pointermove', zone, zx + Math.cos(a) * 60, cy0 + Math.sin(a) * 40);
      }
      await sleep(80);
      log('搅拌点亮星星', $$('#screen-kitchen .stir-star.lit').length === 3);
      click($('#screen-kitchen .btn-magic'));
      await sleep(2300);
      log('烹饪出成品', !$('#screen-kitchen .dish-result').classList.contains('hidden'));
      click($('#screen-kitchen .btn-feed')); // 喂娃娃 → 回世界
      await sleep(900);
      log('喂食后回世界', isWorldActive() && !$('#screen-kitchen').classList.contains('active'), activeScreen());
      /* 做完饭离开后重进厨房：应回到食谱选择页，而不是残留的成品界面 */
      const stove2 = $('#screen-world .world-fixture[data-act="pot"]');

      pe('pointerup', stove2, sx2, sy2);

      window.showScreen('world');
      await sleep(150);

      /* ---- 8. 衣柜→换装 overlay ---- */
      window.World.jumpTo('wardrobe'); // 换衣间
      await sleep(150);
      const wcab = $('#screen-world .world-fixture[data-act="wardrobe"]');

      pe('pointerdown', wcab, wx2, wy2);
      pe('pointerup', wcab, wx2, wy2);
      await sleep(150);
      log('点衣柜开换装面板', $('#screen-dressup').classList.contains('active') && isWorldActive(), activeScreen());
      const dressTab = [...$$('#screen-dressup .tab-btn')].find(t => t.dataset.cat === 'dress');
      click(dressTab);
      await sleep(50);
      click([...$$('#screen-dressup .item-card')].find(c => c.dataset.key === 'fairy'));
      await sleep(50);
      log('换裙子生效', Store.state.doll.dress === 'fairy', Store.state.doll.dress);
      /* Phase 2 v4：dressup 关闭 = 直接切回世界屏（主屏已删） */
      showScreen('world');
      await sleep(100);
      log('dressup 关闭回世界', isWorldActive(), activeScreen());

      /* ---- 9. 墙纸地板（世界版） ---- */
      /* Phase 2 v4：原"点大门"流程已无意义（主屏删），直接确认在世界中，跳 bathroom */
      window.World.jumpTo('bathroom'); // 卫生间
      await sleep(150);
      window.World.jumpTo('bathroom'); // 卫生间
      await sleep(150);
      click($$('#screen-world .btn-palette')[0]);
      await sleep(50);
      const swatches = $$('#screen-world .palette-pop .swatch');
      log('墙纸色板9款', swatches.length === 9, swatches.length);
      click(swatches[7]);
      await sleep(80);
      log('卫生间换薄荷瓷砖', Store.state.rooms.bathroom.wall === 7, Store.state.rooms.bathroom.wall);

      /* ---- 10. 室外场景 ---- */
      window.World.jumpTo('yard'); // 院子
      await sleep(180);
      log('跳到院子', window.World.currentRoomId === 'yard');
      const yardCards = $$('#screen-world .item-card');
      log('院子家具卡12件', yardCards.length === 12, yardCards.length);
      log('含秋千/沙坑/帐篷', ['swing', 'sandbox', 'tent'].every(id =>
        [...yardCards].some(c => c.dataset.id === id)));
      log('院子草地地板', Store.state.rooms.yard.floor === 3, Store.state.rooms.yard.floor);
      click([...yardCards].find(c => c.dataset.id === 'swing'));
      await sleep(150);
      log('院子加秋千', Store.state.rooms.yard.items.length === 6, Store.state.rooms.yard.items.length);
      const swingNode = $$('#screen-world .world-room[data-room="yard"] .room-item')[0];
      const [swx, swy] = center(swingNode);
      pe('pointerdown', swingNode, swx, swy);
      pe('pointerup', swingNode, swx, swy);
      await sleep(100);
      log('轻点秋千互动不误删', Store.state.rooms.yard.items.length === 6);
      window.World.jumpTo('park'); // 公园
      await sleep(180);
      log('跳到公园', window.World.currentRoomId === 'park');
      log('公园含滑梯/喷泉/池塘', ['slide', 'fountain', 'pond'].every(id =>
        [...$$('#screen-world .item-card')].some(c => c.dataset.id === id)));
      /* v0.7+：park <g> 含透明 hit-test 兜底 <rect>，避免 PNG 透明区 click 穿透到街道 */
      log('park <g> 含透明 hit-test 兜底（避免 PNG 透明区 click 穿透）',
        /fill="none" pointer-events="all"/.test($('#screen-map .map-room[data-room="park"]').outerHTML),
        `park-outerHTML=${$('#screen-map .map-room[data-room="park"]').outerHTML.slice(0, 200)}`);
      window.World.jumpTo('shop'); // 商店
      await sleep(180);
      log('跳到商店', window.World.currentRoomId === 'shop');
      log('商店糖果墙默认', Store.state.rooms.shop.wall === 8, Store.state.rooms.shop.wall);
      log('商店含货架/收银台/冰柜', ['shopshelf', 'counter', 'freezer'].every(id =>
        [...$$('#screen-world .item-card')].some(c => c.dataset.id === id)));
      /* v0.7+：shop <g> 含透明 hit-test 兜底 <rect>，避免 PNG 透明区 click 穿透到街道 */
      log('shop <g> 含透明 hit-test 兜底（避免 PNG 透明区 click 穿透）',
        /fill="none" pointer-events="all"/.test($('#screen-map .map-room[data-room="shop"]').outerHTML),
        `shop-outerHTML=${$('#screen-map .map-room[data-room="shop"]').outerHTML.slice(0, 200)}`);

      /* ---- v0.7：ROOM_SETS 重分配后的抽屉内容验证 ---- */
      window.World.jumpTo('bedroom'); // bedroom（合并后，wardrobe 抽屉已移到这里）
      await sleep(180);
      const wCards = [...$$('#screen-world .item-card')];
      log('卧室不再含钢琴（piano 已从 bedroom 移除）',
        !wCards.some(c => c.dataset.id === 'piano'),
        `cards=${wCards.map(c => c.dataset.id).join(',')}`);
      log('卧室含试衣镜和挂衣架（合并 wardrobe 后）',
        Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.some(it => it.id === 'bmirror') && Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.some(it => it.id === 'towelrack'),
        `has-bmirror=${Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.some(it => it.id === 'bmirror')} has-towelrack=${Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.some(it => it.id === 'towelrack')}`);
      await sleep(180);
      const bCards = [...$$('#screen-world .item-card')];
      log('卧室不再含钢琴（piano 已从 bedroom 移除）',
        !bCards.some(c => c.dataset.id === 'piano'),
        `cards=${bCards.map(c => c.dataset.id).join(',')}`);
      log('卧室仍含床（核心物品不能丢）',
        bCards.some(c => c.dataset.id === 'bed'),
        `has-bed=${bCards.some(c => c.dataset.id === 'bed')}`);
      window.World.jumpTo('living'); // living
      await sleep(180);
      const lCards = [...$$('#screen-world .item-card')];
      log('客厅不再含钢琴（piano 已从 living 移除）',
        !lCards.some(c => c.dataset.id === 'piano'),
        `cards=${lCards.map(c => c.dataset.id).join(',')}`);
      log('客厅仍含电视（核心物品不能丢）',
        lCards.some(c => c.dataset.id === 'tv'),
        `has-tv=${lCards.some(c => c.dataset.id === 'tv')}`);
      /* 迁移测试：手动给 bedroom push 一个 piano 和 sofa（不在新 ROOM_SETS 里），
         调 migrateRoomSets 后应该被清掉。 */
      Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.push({ id: 'piano', x: 0.5, y: 0.7 });
      Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.push({ id: 'sofa', x: 0.3, y: 0.5 });
      Store.save();
      const migRes = Store.migrateRoomSets();
      log('迁移：bedroom 里的 piano 和 sofa 被自动清掉',
        !Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.some(it => it.id === 'piano' || it.id === 'sofa'),
        `bedroom-items=${Store.state.rooms.bedroom.items.map(it => it.id).join(',')} dropped=${migRes.dropped}`);
      /* 跳回 shop 继续后续测试 */
      window.World.jumpTo('shop'); // shop
      await sleep(180);
      /* 弹出条跳卧室（键名修复验证） */
      click($('#screen-world .btn-rooms'));
      await sleep(80);
      click(document.querySelector('.room-pick[data-room="bedroom"]'));
      await sleep(200);
      log('弹出条跳卧室生效', window.World.currentRoomId === 'bedroom', window.World.currentRoomId);
      const bedCardVisible = [...$$('#screen-world .item-card')].some(c => c.dataset.id === 'bed');
      log('卧室抽屉已刷新', bedCardVisible);

      /* ---- 10. 存档完整性 ---- */
      Store.saveNow();
      const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('magic-house-v1'));
      log('存档含角色/道具/9房间（wardrobe 合并到 bedroom）', !!(saved.char && Array.isArray(saved.props) && Object.keys(saved.rooms).length === 9));

      /* ---- 11. Phase 2 学校场景样品（仅入库未上线） ---- */
      const items = window.RoomAssets.ITEMS;
      log('Phase 2 样品:blackboard 在 ITEMS 库', !!items.blackboard && /viewBox="0 0 240 180"/.test(items.blackboard.svg), `存在=${!!items.blackboard}`);
      log('Phase 2 样品:ball 在 ITEMS 库', !!items.ball && /viewBox="0 0 130 130"/.test(items.ball.svg), `存在=${!!items.ball}`);
      log('Phase 2 样品:pencilbox 在 ITEMS 库', !!items.pencilbox && /viewBox="0 0 180 110"/.test(items.pencilbox.svg), `存在=${!!items.pencilbox}`);
      log('Phase 2 样品:width 合理', items.blackboard.w === 0.22 && items.ball.w === 0.10 && items.pencilbox.w === 0.16,
        `blackboard=${items.blackboard.w} ball=${items.ball.w} pencilbox=${items.pencilbox.w}`);
      log('Phase 2 样品:中精致特征（描边+多层高光+彩蛋）',
        /stroke=/.test(items.blackboard.svg) && /高光层|高光/.test(items.blackboard.svg) &&
        /stroke-dasharray/.test(items.ball.svg) && /卡扣/.test(items.pencilbox.svg));
      log('Phase 2 样品:未注册到 WORLD_ROOMS（保持 iPad 当前状态）',
        !window.RoomAssets.WORLD_ROOMS.includes('school'),
        `WORLD_ROOMS=${window.RoomAssets.WORLD_ROOMS.join(',')}`);
      log('Phase 2 样品:未进任何 ROOM_SETS（抽屉看不到）',
        !Object.values(window.RoomAssets.ROOM_SETS).some(set =>
          set.includes('blackboard') || set.includes('ball') || set.includes('pencilbox')));
      log('Phase 2 样品:WORLD_ROOMS 仍 9 个（wardrobe 合并到 bedroom）',
        window.RoomAssets.WORLD_ROOMS.length === 9,
        `WORLD_ROOMS.length=${window.RoomAssets.WORLD_ROOMS.length}`);

      /* ---- 13. Phase 2 v4：世界地图 + 房子内部屏（默认屏=地图，主屏删除）---- */
      /* Phase 2 v4：默认屏是地图屏（不再有主屏）。当前应在地图屏或世界屏。
         强制 showScreen('map') 让 Map.onEnter 重新跑一次，刷新角色 marker 位置 */
      showScreen('map');
      await sleep(200);

      /* 地图屏内容 */
      const girlMarker = $('#screen-map .map-marker-girl');
      const catMarker = $('#screen-map .map-marker-cat');
      log('地图屏女娃位置标记存在', !!girlMarker);
      log('地图屏小猫位置标记存在', !!catMarker);

      const girlRoomExpected = Store.state.char.girl.room;
      const catRoomExpected = Store.state.char.cat.room;
      log('地图屏女娃 marker.targetRoom === girl.room',
        girlMarker && girlMarker.dataset.targetRoom === girlRoomExpected,
        `marker=${girlMarker && girlMarker.dataset.targetRoom} store=${girlRoomExpected}`);
      log('地图屏小猫 marker.targetRoom === cat.room',
        catMarker && catMarker.dataset.targetRoom === catRoomExpected,
        `marker=${catMarker && catMarker.dataset.targetRoom} store=${catRoomExpected}`);

      log('地图屏女娃 marker.pulse class 已加', girlMarker && girlMarker.classList.contains('pulse'));
      log('地图屏小猫 marker.pulse class 已加', catMarker && catMarker.classList.contains('pulse'));

      /* 地图屏 1 大房子 + 3 室外（v4 重写后） */
      const houseEl = $('#screen-map .map-house');
      log('地图屏 1 大房子轮廓', !!houseEl);
      const outdoors = $$('#screen-map .map-outdoor');
      log('地图屏 3 室外区', outdoors.length === 3, `n=${outdoors.length}`);

      /* Phase 2 v5：Toca 风格丰富化 —— 拟物数量检查 */
      const mapAllShapes = $$('#screen-map svg *');
      log('地图屏 SVG 子元素 ≥ 90（v7 街道+4 块+拟物丰富）', mapAllShapes.length >= 90, `n=${mapAllShapes.length}`);
      /* L3-3 v5: 大房子改回 apple-touch-icon.png（无 polygon），兼容旧 polygon 检查 + 新 image 检查 */
      log('地图屏大房子含屋顶瓦片',
        $$('#screen-map .map-house polygon').length >= 1 || $$('#screen-map .map-house image').length >= 1);

      /* Phase 2 v7: 3x3 网格 + 街道 */
      const mapBlocks = $$('#screen-map .map-room, #screen-map .map-house');
      log('地图屏 4 块（1 大房子 + 3 室外）', mapBlocks.length === 4, `n=${mapBlocks.length}`);

      /* 街道（深灰柏油） */
      const streetSegs = $$('#screen-map svg rect[fill="#5a5a5a"]');
      log('地图屏有 ≥ 4 段街道', streetSegs.length >= 4, `n=${streetSegs.length}`);

      /* 红绿灯（红圆） */
      const lights = $$('#screen-map circle[fill="#ff5c5c"]').length;
      log('地图屏有 ≥ 4 个红绿灯', lights >= 4, `n=${lights}`);

      /* 路灯（黄灯头） */
      const lamps = $$('#screen-map rect[fill="#ffd34d"][stroke="#a06820"]').length;
      log('地图屏有 ≥ 4 个路灯', lamps >= 4, `n=${lamps}`);

      /* 点大房子 → 直接进世界屏 bedroom（v8 删除 interior 中间层） */
      click(houseEl);
      await sleep(300);
      log('点大房子进世界屏 bedroom',
        activeScreen() === 'screen-world' && window.World.currentRoomId === 'bedroom',
        `screen=${activeScreen()} room=${window.World.currentRoomId}`);

      /* 室外 3 区直接跳世界屏 */
      click($('#screen-map .map-outdoor[data-room="park"]'));
      await sleep(300);
      log('地图点公园直接跳世界屏 park',
        activeScreen() === 'screen-world' && window.World.currentRoomId === 'park',
        `screen=${activeScreen()} room=${window.World.currentRoomId}`);

      /* dots 导航已删除 */
      const dotCount = $$('#screen-world .world-dot').length;
      log('世界屏 dots 导航已删除', dotCount === 0, `n=${dotCount}`);

      const dotsBox = $('#screen-world .world-dots');
      log('世界屏 .world-dots DOM 已删', !dotsBox);

      /* room-picker 仍可打开（保留作为补充入口） */
      click($('#screen-world .btn-rooms'));
      await sleep(80);
      const picker = $('#app .room-picker');
      log('room-picker 仍可打开（保留）',
        picker && !picker.classList.contains('hidden'),
        `hidden=${picker && picker.classList.contains('hidden')}`);

    } catch (err) {
      results.push('FAIL | 测试异常中断 | ' + (err && err.message) + (err && err.stack ? ' | ' + err.stack.split('\n')[1] : ''));
    }
    const fails = results.filter(r => r.startsWith('FAIL')).length;
    results.unshift(`==== 测试完成：${results.length} 项，失败 ${fails} 项 ====`);
    if (window.__errs.length) results.push('JS错误: ' + window.__errs.join(' || '));
    $('#test-result').textContent = results.join('\n');
    document.title = fails === 0 ? 'ALL_PASS' : `FAIL_${fails}`;
  }
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => setTimeout(run, 150));
})();
</script>
